Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The potentials operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 39. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 384 with 4 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_39(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 39. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^384(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 218.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For structural decomposition pairs (A,B) in potentials theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 18 and the invariant I(A,B) = 249 mod 3.

For the inductive step, we decompose the potentials structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 249. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 3. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
